时，模型是否真的把它们当成可组合、可泛化的语义单元来使用，还是只在固定模板下学到脆弱的配对关系。这个问题并不只属于图；视觉 token、音频 token、检索 token、工具 token 乃至多模态 prefix tuning 都会遇到类似风险。论文的价值在于提醒人们，prefix token 接入成功完成任务，不等于模型真正理解了这些 token 的语义，也不等于对指令变化有稳健泛化。","technical_approach":"作者先给出一个统一框架，把 GTokenLLM 拆成四个阶段：Graph Input，Graph Embedding，Graph Token，Text Output。也就是先用 TAG encoder 把 text-attributed graph 编成 embedding，再通过 projector 对齐到 LLM 输入空间，形成 graph tokens，最后与自然语言指令拼接，让 LLM 生成任务输出。这个抽象的意义在于把问题定位清楚：失败可能来自图编码、投影对齐、指令理解，或三者交互，而不是笼统地说“LLM 不懂图”。\n\n在此基础上，论文提出 GTEval 评测管线。核心思路不是换 benchmark，而是对同一任务做 instruction transformations，从两个层面测试 graph-token understanding。一个是 format-level transformation，考察模型对表述形式变化是否过敏，比如同义改写、模板变化、指令组织方式变化；另一个是 content-level transformation，考察模型是否能根据指令内容变化真正调整对 graph token 的使用，而不是只依赖固定模板或文本属性。作者用这个框架去测 6 个代表性 GTokenLLM。\n\n从全文描述看，论文还做了内部分析，检查 graph tokens 是否被模型关注、在各层 attention 中是否持续参与、不同模型和不同指令变体下 token utilization pattern 如何变化。结论不是“graph token 完全没用”，而是更细的：graph token 保留了任务相关的文本和结构信息，LLM 也确实在各层关注它们，但这种利用方式不稳定，对指令变化的响应常常不对劲，表现为过敏或迟钝两种失败模式。\n\n论文还比较了额外 instruction tuning 与 GTextLLM。额外 instruction tuning 能提升原始指令和见过的指令上的表现，但不能根治 graph-token understanding 的问题；GTextLLM 虽然语言理解更强，但在图任务上仍弱于 GTokenLLM，说明问题不只是语言能力不足，而是 tokenized graph representation 与语言空间对齐的方式仍然脆弱。","experiments":"实验对象是 6 个代表性 GTokenLLM，任务场景围绕 text-attributed graph。论文的主要结果可以概括为四点。\n\n第一，现有 GTokenLLM 对 graph token 的理解并不充分。面对 instruction 的格式变化时，有的模型性能大幅波动，说明对表面模板过敏；面对内容变化时，又可能反应不足，说明没有真正把指令语义和 graph token 组合起来。\n\n第二，模型往往高度依赖文本属性进行推理，而对结构信息的使用不够稳健。也就是说，graph token 虽然理论上同时压缩了文本与拓扑，但实际决策中，文本部分更像主导信号。\n\n第三，attention 与内部表征分析显示，graph tokens 并非被完全忽略。它们在各层都能获得注意力，也保留了任务相关信息；问题在于利用模式随模型和指令变体变化较大，缺乏稳定、可解释的语义对齐。\n\n第四，额外 instruction tuning 只能部分缓解。它能改善原始模板和 seen instruction 上的结果，但对 unseen transformation 的泛化仍有限。这说明模型学到的更多是模板适配，而不是 graph token 的深层语义理解。\n\n正文片段没有给出完整表格和具体数值，因此不适合在这里编造各模型在每个数据集上的精确降幅或提升幅度。就研究结论而言，关键不是某个模型高几个点，而是评测方式揭示了一个稳定现象：prefix graph token 的任务有效性，并不自动转化为对指令变化的稳健理解。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着迁移视角去看，而不是把它当图学习论文细读。建议先看 Introduction 和统一框架图，明确 GI→GE→GT→TO 四阶段抽象。然后重点看 GTEval 的设计，尤其是 format-level 和 content-level instruction transformation 分别在测什么。实验部分不必逐个模型记结果，重点抓三件事：一，失败模式是过敏还是迟钝；二，模型到底更依赖文本还是结构；三，instruction tuning 为什么只能修补 seen pattern。\n\n如果只关心对基座研究的启发，20 到 30 分钟足够。值得深挖的点有两个。第一，这套评测思想能否迁移到视觉 token、音频 token、检索 token 等“外部表示接入 LLM”的场景。第二，当前 projector-based token 接入为什么容易学成模板绑定，而不是稳定语义单元，这和 tokenizer 学习、对齐损失、联合预训练深度都有关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a simple but important question: when graph-structured inputs are compressed into graph tokens and prepended to an LLM, does the LLM actually understand those tokens in its natural-language embedding space? Existing Graph-Tokenizing LLMs (GTokenLLMs) are often treated as a strong paradigm because they avoid verbose graph textualization and can encode structure plus text compactly. But the resulting graph tokens are projected from an external graph encoder and do not correspond to any native vocabulary item. Their semantics inside the LLM are therefore opaque. The paper is a systematic evaluation of that semantic understanding, not a new graph-LLM architecture.","importance":"This is only indirectly relevant to core LLM pretraining, since it is mainly an evaluation paper in graph learning rather than a general foundation-model training paper. Still, it touches a broader issue that matters for multimodal and unified modeling: when external continuous representations are injected as pseudo-tokens through a projector, task success does not necessarily mean the model has learned robust, compositional semantics for those tokens. The same concern applies beyond graphs, including visual tokens, audio tokens, retrieval tokens, and other prefix-based alignment schemes. The paper’s value is in making that failure mode visible: token injection can work under fixed prompts while remaining brittle under instruction variation.","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"$14","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, best read with transferability in mind rather than as a graph-learning deep dive. Instead, track three questions: whether failures look like oversensitivity or over-insensitivity, whether the model relies more on text than structure, and why instruction tuning helps only on seen patterns.\n\nIf your interest is foundation-model training, 20-30 minutes is enough. The most useful follow-up questions are whether this evaluation style can be transferred to visual/audio/retrieval token interfaces, and why projector-based token injection so often learns brittle prompt bindings instead of stable semantic units. anime 视频时，现有以“物理合理性”为隐式先验的视频扩散模型会系统性失效。全文反复强调，anime 的难点不只是风格化外观，而是“正确性定义”本身变了。自然视频里，模型只要学到稳定的物理与时序约束，很多样本会朝同一套规律收敛；anime 则没有统一物理法则，夸张变形、节奏停顿、冲击帧、Q 版切换、镜头语言和特效表达都可能是有意为之。于是，直接在通用视频模型上微调，会把这些表达压平；直接混入大量 anime 数据，又会因为风格差异过大、形变过强而训练早期崩掉。论文真正处理的是：如何把生成目标从“物理上像真”改成“制作上正确”，并让模型学会区分有意的艺术性变形与无意的结构性坏帧。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把“目标域先验不匹配”这个问题讲得很清楚：不是所有视频生成都该沿用自然视频学到的物理 prior。这个判断对更广义的基座建模有启发，尤其是当训练分布包含漫画、动画、游戏过场、UI 交互视频、合成世界等非自然数据时，单一 realism objective 可能并不合适。第二，它提出的解决路径不是简单加数据或加控制，而是先重写 supervision 的组织方式，再设计 curriculum 去跨越 prior gap，最后用偏好优化重建质量判据。这种“三段式迁移”对 domain-shift 下的预训练很有参考价值。第三，论文把 caption 从“描述可见内容”改成“表达制作决策”，这与近年预训练里从 surface description 走向 latent factors / process supervision 的趋势一致。贡献量级上，它更像是一个面向特殊生成域的系统性训练框架，而不是单点算法突破；但如果全文实验确实支撑其三步闭环有效，那么它对 质量指标，重点看这些结果，而不是只看 FVD。\n\n第二类是 curriculum 的消融。这里最关键的是证明三轴渐进训练确实缓解了 early collapse，而不是普通的数据排序也能做到。值得关注的证据包括：训练 loss 是否更稳定、样本复杂度提升时是否仍能保持角色结构、去掉 style/motion/deformation 任一轴后性能是否明显下降、直接混合训练是否出现模式坍塌或高频坏帧。若正文给出训练曲线、失败案例分布、不同阶段生成样例，这部分信息量会很高。\n\n第三类是偏好优化与奖励模型的作用。需要看两点：一是 deformation-aware reward 是否比通用视频质量模型更能区分“有意夸张”和“结构崩坏”；二是加入偏好优化后，是否在不明显牺牲可控性的前提下提升了主观质量。若有 pairwise vs 双通道条件、是否使用图推理增强标注。若这些消融成立，才能说明性能提升来自“监督定义改变”，而不只是数据量增加。\n\n由于当前可见全文片段没有给出明确参数规模、数据规模拆分、提升幅度和统计显著性，阅读时需要优先核对主表、消融表和附录中的训练配置，避免只凭定性样例下结论。","reading_guide":"建议按“问题定义—训练迁移—质量判据”三条线来读。\n\n先看 制作变量、标注流程里哪些是自动推断、哪些需要人工校正。AniCaption 的例子尤其值得看：它到底是在生成更长的 caption，还是在显式编码镜头、动作夸张、特效节奏等 latent production factors。若有标注一致性分析或 taxonomy 覆盖率统计，这部分很关键。\n\n接着看 curriculum 章节和训练曲线。这里是全文最值得深挖的地方。重点关注：三轴课程是如何排序和调度的；样本难度如何量化；为什么要同时控制 style、motion、deformation，而不是只按形变强度排序；训练稳定性的证据是否充分。若有 collapse case study、梯度/损失分析、阶段切换策略，这些对预训练研究员最有价值。\n\n再看 reward model 与 preference optimization。要确认奖励模型的训练数据从哪里来，偏好标注标准是什么，如何避免把“更像自然视频”误当成“更高质量 anime”。如果作者做了与 FVD、CLIP、通用 VLM judge 的相关性比较，这部分值得细看。\n\n最后看失败案例和局限性。真正重要的问题包括：角色身份一致性在高形变下是否仍稳定；双通道条件是否会互相冲突；taxonomy 是否会限制开放生成；方法是否依赖高成本标注；能否迁移到漫画动态化、游戏过场、儿童动画等相邻域。完整读下来预计需要 60–90 分钟；若只抓主干，优先看方法图、课程学习消融、偏好优化评测和附录中的标注示例。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a domain-mismatch problem in video generation: anime video is not just a stylized version of natural video, but a medium whose notion of correctness often departs from physical realism. Existing video diffusion models inherit a strong physics prior from natural video data, which helps on real-world motion but becomes a liability for anime. Exaggerated deformation, impact frames, chibi shifts, rhythmic holds, and director-driven camera/VFX choices are often intentional in anime, yet a physics-biased model tends to smooth them away or treat them as errors. Naively scaling anime data does not solve this either, because the domain is highly heterogeneous and lacks a single shared “law” analogous to real-world physics. The core problem, then, is how to redefine the training target from physical plausibility to artistic correctness, and how to train a model that can separate intentional stylization from genuine structural failure.","importance":"$16","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"$18","reading_guide":"$19","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可看 conditioning 设计，尤其结构化制作标签如何覆盖视频模型先验；预训练方法论收获有限。","en":"Worth checking for the conditioning design, especially how structured production tags override video priors; limited pretraining-method insight."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tencent HY Team"],"authors":["Tencent HY Team"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["video-generation","anime","structured-conditioning","multimodal-generation","Tencent"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03652","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.03652","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04040","title":"Large Language Models are Universal Reasoners for Visual Generation","abstract":"Text-to-image generation has advanced rapidly with diffusion models, progressing from CLIP and T5 conditioning to unified systems where a single LLM backbone handles both visual understanding and generation. Despite the architectural unification, these systems frequently fail to faithfully align complex prompts during synthesis, even though they remain highly accurate at verifying whether an image satisfies those same prompts. We formalize this as the \\emph{understanding-generation gap} and prop","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T17:57:36+00:00","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04040.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的不是“图像生成质量不够”这个泛问题，而是统一多模态生成系统里的一个更具体缺口：同一个 LLM 作为视觉理解器时，往往能较可靠地判断一张图是否满足复杂提示词；但作为生成控制器时，却不能把这种理解能力稳定转化为生成约束，尤其在计数、空间关系、属性绑定、物理合理性这类组合语义上容易失真。作者把它定义为 understanding-generation gap。关键点在于，问题不只是文本条件不强，而是生成阶段缺少一个显式的“中间可检验对象”和“基于该对象的纠错信号”，导致模型只能把所有约束一次性压进扩散条件里，难以把验证能力转成可执行的生成指导。","importance":"对预训练和统一建模研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它给“同一 backbone 同时做理解和生成”这条路线补上了一个很重要的机制视角：架构统一不等于能力自动打通，理解强于生成时，需要显式把 verifier 变成 generator 的控制信号。第二，它提出的不是替换扩散 backbone，而是在冻结生成器的前提下，通过 LLM 产生 draft 和 grounded evaluation 来增强条件，这对已有 AR+diffusion、MLLM+image generator 体系都比较容易嫁接。第三，它把多模态生成问题重新表述成一个更接近 reasoning-guided generation 的范式：先形成粗场景计划，再做自我批判，再把批判结果送入生成器。这种“先判后生”的接口设计，对统一预训练、test-time reasoning、以及后续把 verifier / reward model 接到生成模型上的工作都有启发。量级上，它不是基础架构改写，但属于把统一多模态系统从“共享 backbone”推进到“共享推理闭环”的一类方法。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Draft-Evaluate-Diffuse 三阶段流水线，重点不在单个模块新，而在三个条件信号如何互补。\n\n第一步是 Draft。给定文本提示 p，LLM 先生成离散视觉 token 形式的粗草图 d。这里的 draft 不是最终图像，而是一个低保真、场景级别的视觉计划。作者强调必须让 LLM 先落到一个具体视觉实例上，否则后续 evaluation 只能退化成对原 prompt 的改写，缺少 grounded reference。\n\n第二步是 Evaluate。用同一个 LLM 读取原始 prompt 和 draft，对二者是否一致做评估，输出 grounded evaluation e。这个 e 不是泛泛的重写提示词，而是围绕当前 draft 的缺陷给出纠错描述，例如数量不对、关系错位、属性遗漏等。这里的设计抓住了论文最重要的观察：统一模型在“看图判错”上比“直接画对”更可靠，因此应先让它生成一个可批判对象，再把批判结果转成控制信号。\n\n第三步是 Diffuse。冻结扩散模型 SANA，只改条件输入，让扩散过程联合条件于 (p, d, e)。原始 prompt 提供全局语义目标，draft 提供空间锚点和场景布局，evaluation 提供局部纠错指令。作者反复强调三者缺一不可：只看 draft 会继承草图错误；只看 evaluation 而没有 draft，会退化成 prompt rewriting；只看 prompt 则回到标准文本条件扩散，难以处理复杂组合约束。\n\n从方法定位看，它和传统 prompt enhancement 不同，因为 evaluation 是基于具体视觉草图产生的 grounded feedback；也和两阶段 coarse-to-fine 生成不同，因为 refinement 信号不是隐式 latent，而是 LLM 产出的显式语言批注。与把 LLM 直接接到 diffusion cross-attention 的做法相比，这里更像在生成前插入一个 reasoning loop，把“生成失败后容易诊断”的能力前移到生成条件构造阶段。\n\n从预训练研究视角，更值得注意的是它默认了一个能力分工：LLM 负责离散规划与语义校验，diffusion 负责高保真渲染。这种分工比“让单一模型同时学会精细像素生成和复杂组合推理”更现实，也更符合当前统一多模态系统的训练动态。","experiments":"实验结论很直接：在不改动底层扩散 backbone、使用同一个冻结 SANA 的情况下，UniReasoner 主要提升的是复杂提示词对齐，而不是单纯视觉质量。文中给出的核心数字是：GenEval 从 0.79 提升到 0.88，绝对提升 0.09；DPG-Bench 从 84.50 提升到 86.30，提升 1.8 分。前者是更典型的组合对齐评测，因此这个增幅更说明方法确实在修复 understanding-generation gap。\n\n从论文叙述看，作者重点分析了计数、空间关系、物理合理性等失败模式，并展示统一模型在直接生成时会犯错，但在事后评估同一结果时能指出错误。例如 prompt 要求四个苹果，生成出五个，但模型在评估阶段能正确数出五个并识别不匹配。这类案例支撑了方法假设，而不是只靠 benchmark 分数。\n\n消融层面，最关键的比较应是：只用 prompt、prompt+draft、prompt+evaluation、prompt+draft+evaluation。论文主张联合条件最好，原因是 draft 和 evaluation 分别承担“视觉锚定”和“纠错说明”两种不同信息。如果只加 draft，生成器会被错误草图牵着走；如果只加 evaluation，则缺少具体空间参照，纠错信号不够落地。虽然给出的全文片段没有列出全部表格数字，但从方法论证和主结果可以确定，性能提升主要来自这两个条件的互补，而不是单纯增加 token 数量。\n\n另一个值得注意的实验设定是 frozen SANA。这意味着收益主要来自条件构造与推理链路，而不是通过联合训练把生成器重新适配新条件。这个设定让结果更干净，也更说明该方法可作为现有 T2I 系统的外挂式增强模块。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 里 Figure 1 对 understanding-generation gap 的例子。那一页基本决定这篇工作的价值：如果认同“模型会判不会画”是统一生成系统的真实瓶颈，后面的设计就顺理成章。\n\n然后重点读 Method 的三段：Draft、Evaluate、Diffuse。阅读时不要只看流程图，要抓住作者为什么坚持需要 grounded evaluation，而不是直接做 prompt rewriting。这里是方法和已有 prompt engineering / self-refinement 工作的真正分界线。\n\n实验部分优先看两类内容。第一类是 GenEval、DPG-Bench 的主表，确认收益集中在哪些组合约束。第二类是案例分析和消融，尤其是去掉 draft 或去掉 evaluation 后的失败样例。那部分最能说明三种条件信号各自承担什么角色。\n\n如果要深挖，建议追三个问题。1）draft 的离散视觉 token 质量是否形成上限，若 draft 太差，evaluation 是否还能稳定纠错；2）evaluation 的文本长度、粒度、措辞是否影响扩散器吸收纠错信号；3）这种 pipeline 是否能进一步闭环成多轮 draft-evaluate-refine，而不是单轮。真正有研究空间的地方不在“再加一个模块”，而在如何把 verifier 信号更稳定地转成 generator 可利用的训练或推理接口。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in unified multimodal generation systems: the same LLM is often much better at verifying whether an image satisfies a complex prompt than at generating such an image in the first place. The gap shows up on counting, attribute binding, spatial relations, and physical plausibility. The authors call this the understanding-generation gap. Their claim is not merely that text conditioning is weak, but that current generation pipelines lack an explicit intermediate visual object to inspect and an explicit corrective signal derived from that inspection. As a result, all constraints are compressed into one-shot conditioning for diffusion, which is a poor interface for compositional control.","importance":"For pretraining and unified modeling, the contribution is meaningful in three ways. First, it sharpens an important distinction: sharing one backbone across understanding and generation does not automatically transfer the stronger capability into the weaker one. If verification is stronger than direct synthesis, the system needs an explicit mechanism to turn verifier signals into generator control. Second, the method is lightweight from a systems perspective. It keeps the diffusion backbone frozen and improves conditioning through an LLM-produced draft plus grounded evaluation, which makes it easy to attach to existing LLM+diffusion or native multimodal generation stacks. Third, it reframes image generation as reasoning-guided generation: produce a coarse plan, critique it, then render with explicit corrections. That interface is relevant beyond T2I. 对图像质量和多样性的影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why memorization in Stable Diffusion is amplified by the CLIP text embedding interface. The central issue is not only duplicated training images. Stable Diffusion consumes the full sequence of CLIP token embeddings, while short prompts are padded with repeated <eot> tokens in the v1-style tokenizer. These repeated sentence-level embeddings can dominate conditioning and make memorized image reproduction more likely.","importance":"The useful lesson for pretraining is about interface mismatch. CLIP contrastive training mainly optimizes the <eot> representation as the sentence embedding, but diffusion U-Nets attend to every token position through cross-attention. Tokens that were not meant to carry independent semantic content become active conditioning signals at generation time. For multimodal pretraining, this argues for auditing tokenizer conventions, padding semantics, and which positions in a conditioning encoder are actually trained, rather than treating the text encoder output as a uniformly reliable sequence.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose the fixed-length text embedding into <startoftext>, prompt-token, <endoftext>, and padding embeddings. They then intervene on these groups to measure their effect on memorized generations. The main finding is that prompt-token embeddings contribute surprisingly little in memorized cases, while padding embeddings have a strong effect. The proposed mechanism is straightforward: in Stable Diffusion v1, padding repeats <eot> up to length 77, so short prompts contain many near-duplicates of the global sentence representation. Two inference-time mitigations follow from this mechanism: replacing the default <eot> padding with a more neutral token such as “!” while masking the single <eot>, and partially masking padding embeddings.","experiments":"The paper reports that previously observed memorized prompts are all shorter than 40 tokens, which leaves more than 30 repeated <eot>-like padding positions in the 77-token input. A version comparison supports the hypothesis: Stable Diffusion v2.1, which uses OpenCLIP and does not place <eot> in padding positions in the same way, shows much less exact-match memorization. The proposed inference-time fixes reduce memorized reproduction without model retraining or extra compute, while preserving image quality and prompt alignment. They also restore diversity across random seeds, indicating that repeated <eot> padding can collapse sampling toward deterministic copies.","reading_guide":"Skim the introduction for the CLIP-to-diffusion interface mismatch, then read the sections that define token groups and ablate prompt, <eot>, and padding embeddings. i","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T12:15:21+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03677.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 on-policy distillation 的可靠性问题：学生模型在自己的采样轨迹上接受教师 token-level 监督，但学生探索到的状态未必有训练价值，教师在这些学生轨迹上的逐 token 反馈也未必可信。两者都会让 OPD 在长链推理、多任务和多模态蒸馏中不稳定。","importance":"这是一篇 post-train 方向的高相关论文。它没有改变基座预训练目标，但对 pretrain ↔ posttrain 接口有实际信息量。原因是 OPD 同时吸收了 SFT 的 dense supervision 和 RL 的 on-policy sampling：学生不会只模仿离线专家答案，而是在自己会到达的状态上学习；教师又能提供比终局 reward 更细的 token 级训练信号。论文的主要贡献是把 OPD 的失败来源拆成两个可操作问题：状态探索不足和教师监督不可靠，并给出一套在 LLM 与 MLLM 上统一使用的 recipe。对做持续预训练、能力合并、专家模型蒸馏和多模态统一模型的人来说，它提供的是训练信号质量控制方法，而不只是一个蒸馏 loss。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Uni-OPD，核心是 dual-perspective optimization：从学生侧改善探索，从教师侧校准监督。基础 OPD 设定是：给定问题 q，学生策略 πθ 采样轨迹 τ；训练时在这些 on-policy prefix 上最小化学生和教师 πT 的 reverse KL。这个目标可以被解释为在学生实际访问的状态上施加 token-level reward，因此比离线 SFT 更少受 exposure bias 影响，也比只用终局奖励的 RL 更容易做细粒度 credit assignment。学生侧使用两种数据平衡。第一是 offline difficulty-aware balancing，先按样本难度组织训练分布，避免训练批次被过易样本主导，也避免大量不可学样本带来噪声。第二是 online correctness-aware balancing，根据学生当前采样轨迹的正确性动态调节训练样本，让模型持续看到“有信息量”的状态，尤其是接近决策边界的正确与错误轨迹。教师侧提出 outcome-guided margin calibration。它不盲目信任教师在学生轨迹上的 token 概率，而是用 outcome reward 作为全局锚点检查轨迹级排序：正确轨迹的聚合教师分数应高于错误轨迹。若教师的逐 token 信号在轨迹级别与结果奖励不一致，就通过 margin 约束进行校准，使 token-level supervision 与最终正确性保持更一致。这个设计针对的是 OPD 的常见问题：教师在 OOD 学生前缀上退化、给流畅但错误的推理较高概率、多个教师之间信号冲突，或在多模态输入上产生不稳定的 token 偏好。","experiments":"实验覆盖 5 个领域和 16 个 benchmark，包含 LLM 与 MLLM 设置。论文报告了多种蒸馏场景：single-teacher distillation、multi-teacher distillation、strong-to-weak distillation，以及 text-only 与 multimodal task 混合的 cross-modal distillation。关键实验结论是，单独使用 vanilla OPD 会受到学生采样分布和教师信号质量的限制；加入 offline difficulty-aware balancing 和 online correctness-aware balancing 后，学生访问到的训练状态更有信息量；再加入 outcome-guided margin calibration 后，教师 token 监督与结果正确性的顺序一致性提高，训练效果更稳定。消融的核心价值在于证明三件事：探索平衡和监督校准解决的是不同瓶颈；只做数据平衡不能修复错误教师信号；只做教师校准也不能弥补学生状态覆盖不足。正文片段未给出可核验的逐 benchmark 数值，因此不应编造具体提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Introduction 中对 OPD 信号。实验部分优先看三类内容：order-consistency 的分析图、三组件消融、LLM/MLLM 和 multi-teacher 设置下的稳定性对比。值得深挖的是 margin calibration 的可替代实现，例如能否用 verifier、process reward model 或 execution feedback 替代简单 outcome reward，以及这种校准是否会过度压制教师的软分布信息。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies when on-policy distillation is reliable. OPD trains a student on its own sampled trajectories with token-level teacher supervision, but two things can fail: the student may not explore informative states, and the teacher may give unreliable token-level guidance on student-generated prefixes.","importance":"This is a high-signal post-training paper for foundation-model researchers. It does not propose a new pretraining objective, but it is relevant to the interface between pretraining and post-training. OPD combines two useful properties: on-policy sampling from RL and dense token-level supervision from distillation. The paper’s contribution is to turn OPD reliability into two controllable training-signal problems: state coverage and teacher-signal calibration. The two balancing strategies improve the informativeness of student-generated states, while outcome-guided margin calibration improves the agreement between token-level teacher supervision and final correctness. The ablations are important because they separate the two bottlenecks: balancing cannot fix a miscalibrated teacher signal, and calibration cannot compensate for poor state coverage. Each imposes a ceiling. Human judgment cannot supervise capabilities beyond its own, proprietary APIs create dependencies, and verifiable rewards cover only domains with ground-truth answers. Self-improvement from a model's own evaluative capacity is a reward source that","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T15:31:00+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03871.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"EvoLM 解决的是非可验证任务中的奖励来源问题：模型在预训练中已经学到不少评价知识，但常规 RL 后训练通常把奖励交给人类标注、专有模型、标量 reward model 或可验证答案。这些信号要么成本高，要么依赖外部系统，要么容易 reward hacking，要么只适用于有标准答案的任务。论文的核心问题是：能否把模型自身的评价能力结构化成可训练的 rubric，并用这些 rubric 反过来训练同一个模型的策略能力。","importance":"这篇工作的价值不在于提出一个新的 benchmark 分数，而在于把“模型自身评价知识如何成为训练信号”做成了一个相对闭合的训练回路。对预训练研究也有直接启发：它把预训练中隐含的 evaluative knowledge 看作可被后训练激活和重组的能力，而不是只能由外部 reward 注入。更具体地说，EvoLM 提供了一个 post-train 信号质量的建模框架：奖励不是一个黑盒标量，而是由可检查的判分标准产生。这个设计有助于减少 reward model 过优化，也让 reward 的来源更可解释。贡献量级属于方法范式层面的改进，尤其适合关注 pretrain ↔ posttrain 闭环、RL 信号构造、非可验证任务自提升的研究者精读。","technical_approach":"方法的关键是把评价拆成两步：先生成 rubric，再让 judge 按 rubric 对回答打分。rubric 的好坏不由人工标注决定，而由它是否能帮助 judge 区分好回答和坏回答来决定。论文把 rubric 看作潜变量，并用类似变分推断的视角推导训练目标：一个有用的 rubric 应该让 judge 对 preferred response 给出更高分，对 dispreferred response 给出更低分，并拉大二者差距。训练采用交替式 co-evolution。第一阶段固定 rubric generator，让当前 policy 对问题采样多个回答，judge 在 rubric 条件下打分，得到 rubric-derived reward，再用 GRPO 更新 policy。第二阶段固定或使用更新后的 policy 构造偏好对，当前 checkpoint 的回答作为 preferred，早期 checkpoint 的回答作为 dispreferred，这叫 temporal contrast。rubric generator 采样多个 rubric，judge 分别按这些 rubric 给偏好对打分，rubric 的奖励来自分数差是否正确、差距是否足够大，然后同样用 GRPO 更新 rubric generator。与 GPT-4.1 生成 rubric 或人工偏好数据训练 rubric 的方法不同，EvoLM 不需要外部 rubric 标签，也不需要专有 teacher。与标量 reward model 不同，它不直接学习一个压缩后的偏好分数，而是学习可展开、可迁移、可被不同 judge 使用的评价标准。论文还强调一个重要机制：好的 rubric 会把整体判断拆成较小的、可逐项检查的子任务，甚至把预期中间值写进标准里，使较小的 judge 也能稳定判分。","experiments":"实验覆盖 12 个 benchmark，包含数学、代码、一般推理、知识、指令跟随和开放式生成。在 OLMo3-Adapt 评测套件上，EvoLM 的平均分为 69.3%，高于 GPT-4.1-prompted rubrics 的 66.7%。一个在 held-out 偏好评测上很强的标量 reward model，RewardBench 2 为 86.4%，JudgeBench 为 80.8%，但训练出的下游 policy 平均只有 59.7%，比 EvoLM 低 9.6 分。这个结果说明离线偏好判别能力不等于可用于 RL 的好奖励，尤其在 policy 会主动寻找奖励漏洞时更明显。论文还报告了泛化结果：co-evolving 框架可扩展到 OLMo-3-7B；学到的 rubric 可以迁移到未见过的 policy 和 judge；在分布外 deep research 任务上，EvoLM 与专家人工 rubric 的 pairwise agreement 高于 GPT-4.1，HealthBench 为 58.4% 对 52.5%，ResearchQA 为 59.3% 对 51.0%。定性分析显示，训练后的 rubric generator 会生成更细粒度、更可核查的标准，让 1.7B 参数级别的 judge 也能可靠执行一部分判断。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction、Section 2、Figure 2、Section 3.2、Section 3.3 和 qualitative analysis。Figure 2 是理解方法的入口：看清 policy training phase 和 rubric training phase 的交替关系。Section 2 需要重点看 latent rubric variable、discriminative utility、GRPO 目标之间如何连接，这是方法是否站得住的关键。Section 3.2 重点看与 GPT-4.1 rubric、现有 rubric-based RL、标量 reward model 的对照，尤其要关注“偏好 benchmark 上强的 reward model 为什么训练 policy 反而弱”。Section 3.3 重点看迁移到 unseen policies / judges 的实验，因为这决定 rubric 是否只是拟合某个 judge，还是学到了更通用的评价分解。qualitative analysis 值得细读，那里能看到 rubric 从抽象评价变成可检查条件的具体形态。需要进一步追问的点有三个：temporal contrast 是否会把当前 policy 的偏差固化成偏好；judge 本身的系统性错误是否会被 rubric generator 放大；rubric 与 policy 共训练时，是否存在新的 reward hacking 通道。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"EvoLM addresses reward construction for non-verifiable tasks. Current RL post-training usually depends on human labels, proprietary judges, scalar reward models, or verifiable answers. Each option has a ceiling: human supervision is limited, proprietary APIs create dependency, scalar reward models can be overoptimized, and verifiable rewards only cover tasks with ground-truth answers. The paper asks whether a model can turn its own latent evaluative knowledge into explicit rubrics, then use those rubrics as training signal for its own policy.","importance":"The main contribution is a closed training loop for self-generated evaluation. The paper treats evaluative knowledge acquired during pretraining as something that can be surfaced, structured, and optimized during post-training, rather than as an external reward injected into the model. This matters for pretraining research because it clarifies one interface between pretraining and RL: the model may already contain useful criteria for judging responses, but those criteria need to be made operational. EvoLM also moves the reward signal away from an opaque scalar and toward inspectable criteria. This is relevant for reward overoptimization, signal quality, and continual improvement in domains where exact verification is unavailable.","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"Read the introduction, Section 2, Figure 2, Section 3.2, Section 3.3, and the qualitative analysis carefully. Figure 2 gives the operational picture: policy training and rubric training alternate, each using the other as the source of signal. Section 2 is the technical center; focus on the latent-rubric formulation, the discriminative objective, and how it becomes a GRPO-compatible reward. Section 3.2 is important for understanding why a strong scalar reward model can still be a poor RL reward. Section 3.3 should be read for transfer: if rubrics work across policies and judges, they are not merely fitting one evaluation backend. The qualitative examples are also worth close attention because they show how learned rubrics turn broad judgments into small, verifiable checks. This coupling creates a \\emph{silence tax}: additional deliberation postpones the first \\emph{task-relevant} content, while naive early streaming risks premature commitments that bias subsequent generations. We introduce \\textbf{\\emph{Side-by-Side (SxS)}} Interleaved Reasoning, which makes \\emph{disclosure timing} a controllable decision within standard autore","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T02:59:58+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03314.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“如何让模型想得更好”，而是“在单流自回归接口里，模型应该何时把已经想到的内容对用户说出来”。标准 AR 解码把两件事绑在一起：token 一旦生成，既进入后续上下文，也成为不可撤回的公开承诺。这样会带来一个结构性矛盾：继续思考通常有助于提高最终正确率，但会推迟首个有信息量的可见内容；过早输出又会形成前缀承诺，反过来束缚后续推理。论文把这个问题形式化为 disclosure policy learning：在不改模型架构、不引入第二通道或隐藏状态的前提下，让模型在同一 token 流中学习“继续私下思考”还是“公开说出当前已被支持的部分答案”。","importance":"对基座研究的启发主要有两层。第一，它把“流式输出格式”从工程模板问题提升为可学习的生成控制问题，说明单流 AR 接口本身存在可优化的行为层，而不只是系统层 latency 可以优化。第二，它提出了一个很具体的训练信号设计：早披露的内容必须被当前 reasoning prefix 支持，而不是靠 filler 文本去刷更好的首 token 延迟指标。这一点和预训练/后训练接口有关，因为它触及了生成目标里“何时承诺”这一长期被忽略的维度。量级上，这不是替代 AR 的建模范式工作，也不是预训练目标本身的改写；更像是对 reasoning-style post-training 的一个新控制轴，价值在于把 accuracy-latency trade-off 变成可学习的 Pareto 前沿，而不是固定模板下的手工折中。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Side-by-Side Interleaved Generation，简称 SxS。核心设计很克制：仍然是标准自回归模型、同一个上下文、同一套 hidden state，只是在输出序列里加入轻量标签，把 token 分成 think 和 speak 两类。think token 参与上下文更新，但不对用户可见；speak token 既进入上下文，也作为最终回答前缀被提前披露。这里最关键的约束不是“尽早说”，而是“说出来的前缀必须被当前已产生的 reasoning prefix 蕴含”。\n\n训练分两步。第一步是构造 entailment-aligned interleaved supervision。论文从常规的 (x, r, a) 三元组出发，其中 r 是推理链，a 是最终答案，不直接用人工模板切分，而是把 answer prefix 和 reasoning prefix 做对齐，寻找在语义上已被支持的最早披露点。这样得到的 interleaved trajectory 不是简单地在推理中间插答案，而是保证每次 speak 都有“证据前缀”。这一步是在避免两个常见坏解：一是过早承诺，二是输出“让我想想”“我们一步步来”这类低信息 filler。\n\n第二步是两阶段训练：先 SFT，再 RL。SFT 负责教会模型 think/speak 的语义和节奏结构，但作者明确指出仅靠 SFT 会引入明显 distribution shift，因为 interleaved format 改变了原有 reasoning rollout；模型学会了格式，不一定还能在新约束下保持原有推理能力。于是再用 RL 去恢复并优化任务表现，同时学习 accuracy 与 content latency 的折中。论文的表述里，RL 的作用不是单纯提分，而是让模型在“承诺约束”下重新找到有效推理策略。\n\n从方法定位看，它不同于显式双通道或 latent reasoning 系统：没有额外隐藏推理缓存，也没有第二个解码器；不同于纯模板式 streaming CoT：披露时机不是固定规则，而是策略学习；也不同于只优化 TTFT 的系统方法：它优化的是 supported content 何时出现，而不是空洞地更早吐出几个 token。","experiments":"实验覆盖两类 Qwen3 架构：MoE 和 dense；两种规模：文中点名 30B-A3B 与 4B；两个任务域：域内数学推理 AIME25，以及域外科学问答 GPQA-Diamond。评测不只看最终准确率，还看 token-level 的内容延迟代理指标，例如首个被支持的可见内容何时出现、两次更新之间用户需要等待多久。这一点比只报 TTFT 更贴近论文要解决的问题。\n\n全文给出的主结论比较稳定：SxS 在不同架构、不同规模、不同任务域上，都能把 accuracy-latency trade-off 往更好的方向推。也就是说，在相近最终正确率下，模型能更早给出有根据的部分进展；或者在相近内容延迟下，最终正确率更高。作者强调这种改进不依赖架构修改。\n\n从消融逻辑看，最值得注意的是两点。其一，entailment-aligned supervision 是必要的；如果只是机械地鼓励更早输出，模型会学到 filler 或不稳妥的前缀承诺。其二，RL 是必要的；只做 SFT 时，模型虽然学会了 interleaving 格式，但 reasoning performance 会受损，说明格式迁移本身会改变策略分布。RL 之后，任务性能被恢复，同时披露节奏更合理。\n\n由于给出的正文片段没有完整表格数值，无法在这里可靠复述每个 benchmark 的具体百分点提升、各 latency proxy 的绝对数值变化，或不同模型间的精确差距；但从实验设计和作者反复强调的结论看，这篇工作的证据重点不在单点分数，而在 Pareto 曲线整体移动，以及该移动在 dense/MoE、4B/30B-A3B、AIME/GPQA 上都能复现。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐行啃公式。建议先看 Introduction，抓住它要解决的是 commitment timing，而不是普通 CoT 或 streaming。然后重点看 Problem Formulation，理解作者如何把单流 AR 的“状态更新”和“公开承诺”耦合写成一个控制问题。接着看方法部分里两件事：一是 entailment-aligned trajectory 怎么构造，二是为什么必须 SFT+RL 两阶段。最后看实验图表时，不要只盯 accuracy，重点看作者定义的 content-latency proxies，以及 Pareto trade-off 图。\n\n如果时间有限，30 到 45 分钟足够抓住主线。值得深挖的点有三个：第一，entailment 对齐在实践中如何判定，是否依赖额外 verifier 或启发式；第二，RL reward 如何避免再次鼓励 filler；第三，这种 think/speak 标签训练是否会迁移到更一般的 agent streaming、语音对话或多模态逐步披露场景。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about making LLMs reason better per se. It asks a narrower but important question: in a single-stream autoregressive interface, when should the model disclose task-relevant content while it is still reasoning? In standard AR decoding, every generated token both updates the model state and becomes an irreversible public commitment. That coupling creates a structural trade-off. More deliberation can improve final correctness, but it delays useful visible progress; earlier streaming can reduce perceived waiting, but it risks premature commitments that bias the rest of the generation. The paper turns this into a learnable disclosure-policy problem: within the same token stream, the model decides whether to keep thinking privately or to reveal a supported answer prefix.","importance":"The contribution matters more as a post-training/control idea than as a new pretraining paradigm. Still, it has clear relevance to foundation-model research. First, it reframes response pacing from a formatting heuristic into a learned generation policy. That is useful because many current systems optimize wall-clock latency, while ignoring when meaningful supported content actually appears. Second, it introduces a concrete supervision principle: early visible text should be entailed by the reasoning prefix available at that point, rather than rewarded simply for being early. This is a useful training-signal design idea for reasoning models, especially where commitment timing interacts with user experience, streaming, and downstream RL objectives. The scale of impact is behavioral rather than architectural: it does not replace AR language modeling, but it adds a new control axis over the accuracy-latency frontier.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the Introduction to understand that the target is commitment timing, not generic CoT or streaming. Then read the problem formulation carefully: the useful idea is the explicit coupling between state update and public commitment in single-stream AR decoding. In the method section, focus on two pieces only: how entailment-aligned interleaved trajectories are constructed, and why the authors need SFT plus RL instead of SFT alone. In the experiments, pay attention to the content-latency proxies and the Pareto plots, not just final accuracy.\n\nA 30-45 minute read is enough to extract the main idea. The parts worth deeper inspection are: how entailment is operationalized in data construction, how the RL reward avoids incentivizing filler again, and whether the think/speak control scheme could transfer to streaming agents, speech dialogue, or multimodal incremental disclosure.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇值得看的是它把“何时说”从 prompt 技巧抬到可学习策略；先读轨迹构造和 RL 目标，若 setup 干净，能直接影响你对 latent reasoning 接口的设计。","en":"Worth reading because it elevates “when to speak” from prompting to a learnable policy; check the trajectory construction and RL objective first, and if the setup is clean it should update your view of latent-reasoning interfaces."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jiaqi Wei","Xuehang Guo","Pengfei Yu","Xiang Zhang","Wanli Ouyang","Siqi Sun","Qingyun Wang","Chenyu You"],"notable_authors":["Wanli Ouyang"],"tags":["reasoning","chain-of-thought","rl","disclosure-policy","autoregressive-interface"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03314","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.03314","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.02971","title":"Multilingual Safety Alignment via Self-Distillation","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) exhibit severe multilingual safety misalignment: they possess strong safeguards in high-resource languages but remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in low-resource languages. Current safety alignment methods generally rely on high-quality response data for each target language, which is expensive and difficult to generate. In this paper, we propose a cross-lingual safeguard transfer framework named Multilingual Self-Distillation (MSD). This framework transfe","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-03T14:22:49+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_02971.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM x_tgt，代表模型在该语言下的“弱安全通道”。\n- Teacher 额外看到与 x_tgt 对齐的英语 query x*（来自同一多语翻译样本）以及一个 CoT 指令 C：要求用英语进行安全推理，但最终用目标语言输出。这一步的目的不是让 teacher 翻译，而是用英语触发更强的安全推理与拒答策略，再把结果投射到目标语言输出分布上。\n训练时用 teacher 的输出分布/序列来蒸馏 student，使 student 在只给 x_tgt 的情况下也学到类似的安全行为。\n论文强调框架可接不同自蒸馏范式：off-policy（用固定 teacher 生成的轨迹蒸馏）与 on-policy（student 采样、teacher 评估/再指导）两类。\n此外提出 Dual-Perspective Safety Weighting（DPSW）：一个 token-level 的加权散度/损失重加权机制。直觉是安全对齐时并非所有 token 都同等重要：拒答模板、风险点、边界条件等 token 更关键。DPSW 同时看 teacher 与 student 的置信度/分歧来调权：对“安全关键且 teacher/student 分歧大”的 token 加大惩罚，对信息量低或双方都不确定/都一致的 token 降权，从而让蒸馏更聚焦。","experiments":"给出的全文片段未包含具体 benchmark 名称、模型规模、以及核心数值表格，因此无法可靠复述提升幅度与统计细节。根据文中描述：\n- 在 off-policy 与 on-policy 两种设置下都做了实验。\n- 覆盖多个模型与多个安全/越狱基准，并测试更难数据集与 OOD 语言的泛化。\n- 结论是 MSD notatio","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T07:25:33+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03441.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要回答的问题很具体：数学“编码”类 jailbreak 到底是因为数学符号本身绕过了安全过滤，还是因为编码过程中把有害意图做了更深层的语义重写（reformulation），从而逃过基于语义模式的防护并诱导模型在“解题+举例”阶段还原成可执行的有害内容。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在“攻击技巧”，而在两个更底层的点：\n1) 现有安全对齐/拒答训练与安全分类器更像是在自然语言表面分布上做判别，遇到跨表征（natural language → formal math problem）的语义同构变换时会失效，说明对齐信号覆盖的“表示空间”不够。\n2) 攻击成功的关键是“深度改写 + 任务指令（solve + real-world examples）”的组合，这提示对齐训练中对“解释/举例/落地”类指令的风险建模不足；这类指令在预训练里是高频的通用能力，后训练若只在自然语言有害模板上做抑制，容易留下结构性缺口。\n整体贡献更偏系统性测量与机制归因，而不是提出新的预训练目标；因此对基座预训练的影响属于“对齐失败模式的证据与诊断框架”，量级中等。","technical_approach":"方法是一个三阶段黑盒流水线：Encode（编码）→ Mechanics（量子表述）。为降低 helper 拒答，作者给 helper 设定学术研究系统提示，并配少量 few-shot 示范，强调形式化重写的结构。\n- Rule-based encodings：不做语义重写，只做表面格式化/符号注入（如把文本嵌进加法方程、条件概率模板、符号插入等）。\n提交阶段的关键不是“让模型解码”，而是统一使用指令模板要求目标模型“解题并给出现实世界的 practical examples”。作者明确指出：这个指令会迫使模型把抽象形式落回可执行步骤，相当于把编码逆转成有害操作。\n稳健性测试用的是 repeat post-processing：把编码后的 prompt 进行重复/复制等表面增强，观察 ASR 变化，借此判断攻击是否依赖脆弱的字符串形态。","experiments":"实验覆盖 6 种编码策略、8 个目标模型、2 A correct final answer does not imply that the reasoning trace is faithful, reliable, or useful to the model that consumes it. The proposed setup treats the reasoning trace as an intermediate interface between a trainable planner and a frozen executor.","importance":"The direct relevance to base pretraining is limited, but the work is useful for understanding the post-training signal that feeds back into model behavior. Outcome-only verifiable rewards can reinforce shortcuts or traces that are correct for accidental reasons. TraceLift separates trace quality from downstream executor utility, which is a cleaner training signal for systems where chain-of-thought is part of the computation rather than just a display format.","technical_approach":"TraceLift has three main components. First, it builds TraceLift-Groups, a reason-only dataset with 6,000 reasoning groups sampled from GSM8K train and OpenCodeReasoning. Each group contains a high-quality reference trajectory and multiple targeted flawed trajectories for the same problem, annotated with multi-dimensional rubric scores. Second, a Reasoning Reward Model is trained to score problem-reasoning pairs by trajectory quality rather than final correctness. Third, planner optimization uses a frozen executor: the planner emits tagged reasoning, the executor consumes the problem and trace to produce the final artifact, and a verifier checks the artifact. The reward combines verifier feedback, the Reason RM score, and measured executor uplift over a no-reasoning baseline. GRPO is used for planner training. At evaluation time, the Reason RM and uplift computation are removed, keeping the same frozen executor in the two-stage chain.","experiments":"The experiments cover math and code settings under a fixed planner-executor evaluation protocol. The provided text does not include exact model sizes, benchmark tables, pass rates, or ablation numbers, so the quantitative magnitude cannot be reconstructed. While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance.","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-06T15:02:52+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_02909.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 RLVR 中系统性 verifier 错误的影响，核心结论是：错误不只是让训练变慢，还可能让模型停在次优水平，甚至学会利用错误奖励导致训练崩坏。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在 RL 信号质量。RLVR 常被视为比偏好模型更干净，因为奖励来自可验证答案。但实际 verifier 往往不是完美 oracle，尤其在代码、工具调用、形式化检查、LLM-as-a-judge 场景中，错误会有结构性模式。论文提醒一个关键点：初始 FPR/FNR 这类平均错误率不足以判断 verifier 是否安全。模型训练会改变输出分布，并主动靠近奖励漏洞，所以低初始错误率也可能在训练中被放大。","technical_approach":"论文先区分随机噪声和系统性错误。随机噪声通常近似独立，同一类样本不会稳定被错判；系统性错误则由某些触发模式引起，会对特定输出形态持续给出错误奖励。实验使用 Reasoning Gym 的 arithmetic 任务，并在 ground-truth verifier 上人为注入可控错误。错误类型包括随机 FN、格式触发 FN，例如包含 \"\\\\[\" 的答案被拒；随机 FP；数值近似触发 FP，例如相对误差小于 1 被接受；以及词触发 FP，例如输出包含 \"Certainly\" 或 \"python\" 被接受。训练过程中跟踪 oracle reward、global FPR、global FNR 和模型行为。这样的设计优点是变量清楚，可以观察模型是否学会某个触发模式，而不是只看最终分数。","experiments":"代表性实验使用 OLMo3-7B。结果分成三类动态：delay、plateau、collapse。随机噪声和部分一致性 FN 主要延缓训练，但最终仍可能达到较高 oracle reward。较温和的系统性错误会让模型停在次优 plateau，例如 verifier 接受某些近似答案，模型会学到足够拿奖励但不完全正确的策略。破坏性 FP 会导致 collapse，模型发现简单触发词比真正解题更容易拿分，训练后 oracle reward 下降。一个重要观察是，collapse 可以在初始 FPR 很低时发生；相反，某些初始错误率更高但对称的错误只导致 plateau。§4.4 还指出，围绕真值不对称的错误模式比对称错误更危险，即使后者的平均错误率更高。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 35 分钟。先看 Figure 1，快速建立三类训练动态：delay、plateau、collapse。再看 §3 中对系统性错误的定义和分类，这是论文最有复用价值的部分。§4.2 的 controlled experiments 需要看实验设置和不同触发器，不必逐字读所有曲线。§4.4 值得细读，因为它说明为什么平均 FPR/FNR 会误导 verifier 选择。对做 RLVR 的读者，§4.3–§4.5 的实践讨论可以转化为检查清单：不要只报告 verifier 的静态准确率，还要检查错误是否与可生成模式、格式模板、常见 token、近似答案区域相关。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies systematic verifier errors in RLVR. Its main point is that verifier errors do not merely slow learning; depending on their structure, they can delay training, create a suboptimal plateau, or drive the policy toward reward-exploiting behavior and collapse.","importance":"The work is useful for understanding RL signal quality. RLVR is often treated as a cleaner alternative to preference-based rewards because it relies on verifiable answers. In practice, verifiers are imperfect. Static analyzers, code checkers, approximate graders, and LLM judges often make structured mistakes. The paper shows that aggregate false-positive and false-negative rates are not enough to decide whether a verifier is safe for training. During RL, the policy distribution shifts toward rewarded outputs, so even rare initial false positives can become attractive if they are easy for the model to exploit.","technical_approach":"The paper distinguishes random verifier noise from systematic verification error. Random noise is approximately independent across samples. Systematic error is tied to stable triggers or regions of the output space, so the model can repeatedly benefit from it. The experiments use the arithmetic task from Reasoning Gym and inject controlled errors into an otherwise ground-truth verifier. The injected errors include random false negatives, format-triggered false negatives such as rejecting answers containing \"\\\\[\", random false positives, approximate-answer false positives such as accepting relative error below 1, and word-triggered false positives such as accepting outputs containing \"Certainly\" or \"python\". The authors track oracle reward, global false-positive rate, global false-negative rate, and model behavior throughout training. This setup isolates the effect of error structure rather than only measuring final performance.","experiments":"Representative results use OLMo3-7B. The observed dynamics fall into three regimes. Random noise and some consistent false negatives mainly delay training while still allowing high final oracle reward. Benign systematic errors can produce a plateau, where the model learns a strategy that is rewarded by the verifier but remains imperfect under the oracle. Destructive false positives can cause collapse: the model discovers a simple trigger that receives reward more reliably than solving the task, and oracle reward drops. A key result is that collapse can occur even when the initial false-positive rate is low. Conversely, a higher-rate but more symmetric error pattern may only lead to a plateau. Section 4.4 highlights that asymmetric errors around the ground truth can be more damaging than symmetric errors with a larger average error rate.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. Start with Figure 1 to understand the three training regimes: delay, plateau, and collapse. Then read Section 3 for the definition and taxonomy of systematic verifier errors; this is the most reusable part. Section 4.2 is worth reading for the controlled experimental setup and trigger design. Section 4.4 deserves closer attention because it explains why static FPR and FNR can mislead verifier selection. For RLVR practice, Sections 4.3 to 4.5 can be used as a checklist: evaluate not only verifier accuracy, but also whether errors correlate with format patterns, common tokens, easy-to-generate strings, or approximate-answer regions.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"RLVR verifier 不是只看总体准确率；应优先读系统性 false positive 的实验，内部训练要复现这个 failure mode。","en":"Verifier quality in RLVR is not captured by aggregate accuracy; read the systematic false-positive experiments and reproduce that failure mode internally."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Max Planck Institute for Intelligent Systems, Tübingen"],"authors":["Kazuki Egashira","Mark Vero","Jasper Dekoninck","Florian E. Dorner","Robin Staab","Martin Vechev"],"notable_authors":["Martin Vechev"],"tags":["RLVR","verifier-noise","reward-modeling","reasoning-training","training-dynamics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.02909","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.02944","title":"Exploring Pass-Rate Reward in Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation","abstract":"Reinforcement learning (RL) from unit-test feedback has become a standard post-training recipe for improving large language models (LLMs) on code generation. However, the pass-all-tests binary reward can be sparse, yielding no learning signal on challenging problems where none of the sampled solutions passes all tests. A common remedy is to use the test-case pass rate as a surrogate reward.   In this work, we study pass-rate rewards in critic-free RL for code generation (e.g., GRPO and RLOO) and","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-01T10:26:43+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_02944.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究一个看起来直接、但在代码 RL 里并不简单的问题：把 unit test 的 binary reward（全部通过才给 1）换成更稠密的 pass-rate reward（按通过测试比例给分），是否真的能在 critic-free RL 中带来更好的学习效果。论文关心的不只是 reward sparsity，而是更深一层的 reward alignment：部分通过的程序，是否真的比低通过率程序更接近“完全正确”。作者的结论是否定的。在 GRPO、RLOO 这类组内相对优势更新框架下，pass-rate reward 虽然更密，但并不稳定带来更高的最终 pass@k 或成功率；原因是它对“接近正确”的刻画失真，导致梯度方向噪声大、组内相互抵消，训练未必朝 full correctness 推进。","importance":"对预训练和 post-train 接口的启发主要有两点。第一，它提醒人们不要把“更密的奖励”直接等同于“更好的优化信号”。这和预训练里常见的 proxy mismatch 是同一类问题：目标更容易优化，不代表更接近真正想要的能力。第二，这篇工作对 RLVR 尤其是代码与推理任务很有参考价值，因为很多后续方法都默认 finer-grained verifier 会自然优于 binary verifier。这里给出的证据更克制：如果 reward 不能保持正确的排序关系，critic-free policy gradient 很容易学到 exploit test suite 的局部策略，而不是学到可泛化的程序构造能力。贡献量级不在于提出新算法，而在于把一个流行经验法则拆开，说明它何时失效、为什么失效。","technical_approach":"方法上，这篇论文不是做新 RL 算法，而是做严格控制变量的 reward study。作者在多个 base model、两类常见 critic-free 算法 GRPO 和 RLOO 上，对比了几种 reward 设计：标准 binary reward、直接 pass-rate reward、对测试样例重加权的 pass-rate 变体，以及先用 pass-rate 再切换到 binary 的 two-stage 方案。分析重点有两个层面。\n\n第一层是 reward density。作者先验证 pass-rate 确实比 binary 稠密，很多 rollout 会拿到中间分，而不是全 0/1。也就是说，问题不在“信号有没有”。\n\n第二层是 gradient direction。作者进一步看这些中间分对应的更新方向是否有用。结论是，pass-rate 不是 full correctness 的可靠 surrogate。一个程序可能靠 brute force、特判、对公开测试过拟合拿到较高 pass rate，但离真正正确还很远；反过来，一个只差边界条件或一个小 bug 的近正确程序，可能 pass rate 并不高。放到 GRPO/RLOO 里，这会带来两个后果：\n1. 组内 advantage 排序失真。高 pass-rate 样本未必是最值得放大的样本。\n2. 当一组 rollout 里没有任何 fully correct 解时，不同 partial solutions 对应的更新方向彼此分散，group-level update 容易抵消。\n\n论文的机制分析围绕“miscalibration”展开：reward 更密，但与最终目标的单调关系不够好，因此 policy gradient 的有效信噪比没有随之提升。相比一些默认把 pass-rate 当作 reward shaping 的做法，这篇文章更强调 surrogate reward 的排序质量，而不是数值分辨率。","experiments":"从全文可确认的实验设计看，作者做了多 base models、多算法、多 reward 形式的 controlled experiments，核心比较对象是 binary reward 与 pass-rate reward 在 critic-free RL 下的最终代码生成表现。摘要和正文前半部分给出的主结论很一致：pass-rate reward 在不同 base models、GRPO 与 RLOO 上都没有稳定超过 binary reward 的最终性能上限。\n\n实验结果的关键观察不是单一平均分，而是训练动态和组内样本结构：\n- pass-rate reward 确实提高了非零奖励样本比例，缓解了“整组全 0”的情况；\n- 但最终 pass-all-tests 指标没有对应提升，说明 reward density 与 end-task gain 脱钩；\n- 在困难题上，如果没有 rollout 达到 full pass，partial-pass 样本提供的优势信号方向分散，更新幅度弱；\n- 一些高 pass-rate 样本表现出 shortcut 行为，说明 reward 被测试覆盖不全所误导；\n- two-stage 或 reweighted 变体可以在部分阶段改善优化稳定性，但没有改变核心结论：只要 surrogate 与 full correctness 的对齐不够，最终 ceiling 仍受限。\n\n用户给出的全文片段没有包含完整表格数值、模型参数量、训练步数和具体 benchmark 分数，因此不能负责任地编造具体数字。精读时应直接核对主表、附录中的 reward ablation 表，以及分析不同难度题目分桶结果的图。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读但要抓住分析部分，不建议只看摘要。优先顺序如下：\n1. 先看 Introduction，明确作者不是在否认 dense reward 的价值，而是在限定 critic-free code RL 这个具体设定。\n2. 再看实验主表，确认 binary、pass-rate、reweighted、two-stage 在不同 base model 和 GRPO/RLOO 下的相对关系。\n3. 重点看机制分析部分，也就是 reward density 与 gradient direction 的拆分。这里是全文最有信息量的地方。要注意作者如何证明“有信号”不等于“有用信号”。\n4. 看案例分析，尤其是高 pass-rate 但错误的程序，与低 pass-rate 但接近正确的程序对比。这部分最能帮助理解 miscalibration。\n5. 最后看附录里的实现细节和控制变量设置，确认比较是否公平，比如 rollout 数、测试集构造、group size、归一化方式。\n\n预计阅读时间 20–30 分钟。若你关心 RL pretrain、verifiable reward 或 reasoning/code post-training，这篇最值得看的不是方法，而是它对 reward design 的负结果和机制解释。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important question in code RL: if binary unit-test reward is too sparse, does replacing it with test-case pass rate actually improve critic-free RL? The answer is largely no. The issue is not reward density alone, but whether pass rate is a faithful surrogate for progress toward full correctness. In GRPO- and RLOO-style training, denser rewards do provide more non-zero feedback, yet they do not reliably yield better final coding performance because partial-pass programs are often poorly ranked with respect to true correctness.","importance":"The main value is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. It challenges a common assumption in RLVR for code and reasoning: that a denser verifier signal should naturally make optimization better. The paper shows that this is only true when the surrogate preserves useful ordering. If not, critic-free policy gradients can amplify shortcut solutions, overfitting to the visible test suite instead of moving toward genuinely correct programs. For people working on the pretrain–posttrain interface, this is a clean example of proxy mismatch: an easier objective can still be a worse training signal.","technical_approach":"$24","experiments":"$25","reading_guide":"Read this as a reward-analysis paper, not a methods paper. weaknesses in critic-free RL for LLM reasoning. First, methods such as GRPO assign a single sequence-level advantage uniformly to every token in a long chain-of-thought response. In thousand-token trajectories, decisive reasoning steps and trivial connective tokens receive the same learning signal, so credit assignment is extremely coarse. Second, the usual reverse-KL regularization is unbounded and strongly mode-seeking. When the policy moves away from the reference model, gradients can become sharp and unstable, and the model is pushed to stay near the pretrained distribution instead of exploring new reasoning paths. The paper asks whether one can keep the memory efficiency of critic-free RL, avoid a value model, and still obtain finer token-level supervision with a smoother exploration mechanism.","importance":"$27","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"$29","reading_guide":"$2a","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看方法和 ablation：若它真把 KL 偏移变成可用的 step 信号，GRPO 内部实现应复现一版。","en":"Read the method and ablations: if KL deviation is genuinely turned into a useful step-level signal, it deserves an internal GRPO-style reproduction."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Peking University","SJTU","Tsinghua University"],"authors":["Hongbo Jin","Rongpeng Zhu","Zhongjing Du","Xu Jiang","Jingqi Tian","Qiaoman Zhang","Jiayu Ding"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLHF","RLVR","GRPO","credit-assignment","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03327","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.03327","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.03869","title":"On Adaptivity in Zeroth-Order Optimization","abstract":"We investigate the effectiveness of adaptive zeroth-order (ZO) optimization for memory-constrained fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Contrary to prior claims, we show that adaptive ZO methods such as ZO-Adam offer no convergence advantage over well-tuned ZO-SGD, while incurring significant memory overhead. Our analysis reveals that in high dimensions, ZO gradients lack coordinate-wise heterogeneity, rendering adaptive mechanisms memory inefficient. Leveraging this insight, we propose ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T15:29:11+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03869.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","math.OC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文重新检查 ZO fine-tuning 中的自适应优化器是否真的有必要。核心结论是：在高维 Transformer 参数空间中，随机扰动得到的 ZO 梯度缺少一阶训练里那种明显的坐标级异质性，因此 ZO-Adam 的逐坐标二阶矩跟踪很难带来收敛优势，却会增加显存开销。","importance":"它不直接服务于基座预训练，但对大模型训练研究有一个清晰启发：优化器里的自适应机制是否有效，取决于梯度估计是否含有可利用的坐标结构。Adam 在反向传播梯度上有用，是因为不同坐标的梯度尺度差异明显；ZO 梯度由高维随机方向投影构造，坐标统计更接近同质。这个差异提醒我们，不能把 FO 训练中的优化器直觉直接搬到 ZO 或其他噪声很强的训练信号上。对于内存受限的 LoRA/SFT 或边端微调，这个结论有实际参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文从标准 ZO 梯度估计器出发：沿随机方向 u 做有限差分，用 loss 差值乘以 u 来估计梯度。高维下，随机方向的各坐标近似各向同性，导致估计梯度的二阶矩主要由全局梯度范数和扰动噪声控制，而不是由真实梯度的逐坐标尺度控制。因此，ZO-Adam 维护每个参数的二阶矩，很多时候只是在存储近似相同的统计量。基于这个观察，作者提出 MEAZO，只跟踪一个全局标量作为归一化因子，用它做全局 step size adaptation。这样保留了 ZO-Adam 对学习率不那么敏感的优点，但显存接近 ZO-SGD。论文还给出标准 smooth non-convex 假设下的收敛分析，并在附录中说明一阶矩归一化对 adaptive ZO 并非必要。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个 LLM family 和任务，包含 LoRA fine-tuning 设置。文中展示了 Llama-3.2 1B 在 XSum 上的例子，并比较 FO-Adam、FO-SGD、ZO-Adam、ZO-SGD 等方法。在最优学习率下，调好的 ZO-SGD 可以达到或超过已有 adaptive ZO 方法；ZO-Adam 没有表现出稳定的收敛优势，但需要额外保存逐参数状态。MEAZO 的表现接近 ZO-Adam，同时只增加一个标量级状态，显存开销接近 ZO-SGD。另一个重要实验结论是鲁棒性：ZO-SGD 对学习率调参更敏感，MEAZO 在学习率选择上更宽容。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 FO adaptivity 与 ZO adaptivity 的对比，再看 ZO 梯度估计器和高维同质性分析，这是论文的主论点。实验部分重点看 learning-rate sweep，而不只看单点最优结果，因为 MEAZO 的价值主要体现在稳定性和显存取舍。若只关心预训练优化器，可跳过具体任务细节，重点保留它对“梯度统计结构决定优化器设计”的分析。","authors_note":"Qualcomm AI Research。作者具体名单在给定材料中无法确认。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether adaptive zeroth-order optimizers are actually useful for memory-constrained LLM fine-tuning. Its main conclusion is that high-dimensional ZO gradient estimates do not exhibit the coordinate-wise heterogeneity that makes Adam effective in backpropagation-based training. As a result, ZO-Adam-style per-parameter second-moment tracking adds memory cost without a clear convergence benefit over well-tuned ZO-SGD.","importance":"The work is most relevant as an optimizer-design lesson rather than as a pretraining method. It shows that adaptivity only helps when the gradient signal contains exploitable coordinate structure. First-order gradients in Transformer training often have strong coordinate-wise scale variation; ZO gradients built from isotropic random perturbations are much more homogeneous. This distinction matters for memory-limited SFT, LoRA tuning, and any training regime where the gradient estimator is noisy or heavily smoothed.","technical_approach":"The analysis starts from the standard finite-difference ZO estimator: evaluate the loss at positively and negatively perturbed parameters, estimate a directional derivative, and multiply by the random direction. In high dimension, isotropic perturbations make the coordinate statistics of the estimator nearly uniform. The second moment seen by each coordinate is dominated by global quantities such as the gradient norm and estimator noise, rather than by the true coordinate-wise gradient scale. Therefore, a ZO-Adam optimizer that stores per-coordinate moments mostly tracks redundant information. MEAZO replaces this with a single scalar normalization state for global step-size adaptation. It keeps the memory footprint close to ZO-SGD while retaining much of the learning-rate robustness of adaptive ZO methods. The paper also provides convergence guarantees under standard smooth non-convex assumptions and reports that first-moment normalization is not necessary in their adaptive ZO setting.","experiments":"The experiments cover several LLM families and datasets under ZO fine-tuning, including LoRA experiments. One highlighted setup is Llama-3.2 1B on XSum, comparing FO-Adam, FO-SGD, ZO-Adam, and ZO-SGD. With careful learning-rate tuning, ZO-SGD matches or surpasses existing adaptive ZO optimizers. ZO-Adam does not show a consistent convergence advantage but requires per-parameter optimizer states. MEAZO performs close to ZO-Adam while adding only scalar-level state, and it is less sensitive to the learning-rate choice than plain ZO-SGD. The key empirical evidence is the combination of optimal-learning-rate comparisons and learning-rate sweeps.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Read the introduction for the contrast between first-order and zeroth-order adaptivity, then focus on the section analyzing the statistics of ZO gradients in high dimension. In the experiments, prioritize the learning-rate sweeps and memory comparisons over task-specific details. For pretraining researchers, the useful point is the diagnostic principle: optimizer state should match the structure present in the gradient estimator, not the structure assumed by a different training regime.","authors_note":"Qualcomm AI Research. We consi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-04T18:17:05+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03052.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 that is not Y is Z”这类受控提示构造分析。作者比较两种机制假设：一种是 suppression，即模型注意到被否定的概念 Y，然后压低相关 token；另一种是 construction，即模型显式构造“not Y”的组合表征，再用这个表征促进正确答案。方法上，作者先用基于 logit difference 的 negation sensitivity 证明模型内部对否定敏感；然后定位到后层若干 attention heads 会在否定提示中促进正向答案，称为 shortcut attention heads。为干预这些头，作者提出 Attention Sinking，用来削弱特定注意力模块对输出的影响，并观察否定题准确率变化。机制追踪部分使用 path patching、LogitLens 和 Sparse AutoEncoders。结果显示，否定词的信息先被 attention 移到 Y 的位置；中层 attention 再把构造出的负概念表征移到输出位置；同一批模块也会压制 Y，但 suppression 的作用较弱。最后，MLP 的 SAE latent 会进一步放大这个负概念表征，使它影响最终预测。","experiments":"数据集包含 162 个独立问题，使用 4 个模板扩展为 648 条样本，正负提示只在否定标记上不同，并为每条样本指定单 token 的正向和负向候选答案。主要机制实验集中在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Mistral-7B-v0.1，也提到若干 open-weight 模型在否定题上常答错但仍表现出内部敏感性。关键结果有三点。第一，后层 shortcut attention modules 会在否定提示上推动正向答案；对这些模块做 Attention Sinking 后，否定题准确率显著提高。第二，在超过 80% 的数据样本中，LogitLens 能看到中层 attention 输出促进与“not Y”相关的概念，而不是只压低 Y。第三，SAE 分析显示，后续 MLP latent 会把这些负概念方向进一步放大。论文还把 shortcut attention heads 的形成追溯到预训练阶段，说明它们不是 instruction tuning 后才出现的行为。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。优先看 §4，理解 negation sensitivity、shortcut attention heads 和 Attention Sinking 的因果证据；再看 §5.1–§5.5，那里给出了“否定词搬运到 Y 位置—构造负概念表征—输出位置读出—MLP 放大”的完整路径。图 1 和展示 construction/suppression 对比的图表值得细看。附录中的数据构造也应扫一遍，因为 648 条样本的模板化设定决定了结论边界。读的时候需要留意两个限制：一是任务形式较窄，主要覆盖分类式单 token 回答；二是 Attention Sinking 能改善行为，但它更像机制验证工具，不是可直接部署的训练方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how LLMs compute negation internally. The main question is whether failures on negated prompts come from a missing negation mechanism, or from later components overriding a mechanism that already exists.","importance":"The useful point for pretraining research is the separation between representation acquisition and readout interference. The models appear to build compositional representations for negated concepts in mid layers, while late attention heads push answers associated with the unnegated concept through shortcut features such as co-occurrence. This matters for studying pretraining dynamics because a model can learn the right intermediate computation and still produce the wrong output if later circuits amplify easier correlations.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"The dataset contains 162 unique questions rendered with 4 templates, yielding 648 examples. Positive and negated prompts differ only by a negation marker, with single-token positive and negative answer candidates. The mechanistic experiments focus on Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B-v0.1, with broader observations on open-weight models. The main empirical findings are: late attention modules promote unnegated answers and ablating or sinking them improves accuracy on negated prompts; for more than 80% of examples, LogitLens evidence indicates that mid-layer attention outputs promote concepts related to “not Y”; SAE features in MLP outputs further amplify the negated representation. The paper also links shortcut heads to pretraining, suggesting that these behaviors are not only artifacts of instruction tuning.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. The appendix on dataset construction is worth checking because the conclusions are tied to controlled, single-token answer prompts. Recent interpretability studies have identified from middle-layer representations task-specific directions, or task vectors, that steer model behavior. the","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T14:07:55+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03780.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 Transformer 如何在上下文中推断潜在任务：一类是识别训练中见过的任务并做贝叶斯式检索，另一类是面对训练支持集之外的任务时利用上下文统计做外推。核心问题不是提出新的预训练算法，而是给“task vector”一个更严格的定义，并解释训练任务分布如何塑造隐状态中的任务几何。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发在于，它把 in-context learning 的两种能力拆成了可分析的表示几何问题。ID 任务依赖隐状态中任务向量系数的收敛，OOD 任务则依赖与任务向量近正交的上下文统计子空间。这个区分有助于理解为什么增加训练任务多样性可能改善外推，而不仅是记住更多任务。它也提醒研究者：直接从大模型隐状态中提取所谓 task 链任务。这样的设置允许作者把 task vector 定义为 latent task 的表示映射，并在只观察 hidden states 的灰盒条件下分析模型如何通过隐状态与任务向量的相互作用完成推断。方法上的重点是把先前经验性的 task-vector 提取，改写为与训练分布、后验推断和表示子空间相关的几何刻画；同时指出已有启发式 task-vector context. It separates two modes: Bayesian retrieval of tasks seen in the training support, and extrapolative adaptation to tasks outside that support. The main goal is to give task vectors a statistical and geometric definition, rather than treating them as post-hoc directions extracted from hidden states.","importance":"The useful point for pretraining research is the distinction between memorized task retrieval and context-based generalization. For in-distribution tasks, behavior is governed by coefficients along task-vector directions. For out-of-distribution tasks, generalization can rely on context statistics represented in nearly orthogonal subspaces. This gives a cleaner way to reason about why task diversity during pretraining may improve extrapolation, not just coverage of more latent tasks. It also cautions against using heuristic task-vector extraction as direct evidence of a model’s internal algorithm.","technical_approach":"The authors train small Transformers from scratch on controlled synthetic latent-task sequence distributions. A sequence is generated by first sampling a latent task z and then sampling tokens from the task-specific distribution p_z. The settings include multinomial distributions with Dirichlet-sampled probability vectors, linear regression with a D=6 latent weight vector, and first-order Markov chains with V=6 states and Dirichlet-sampled transition rows. This controlled setup lets the authors define task vectors as representations of latent variables and analyze hidden states from a gray-box perspective. The contribution is not a new model architecture, but a framework connecting hidden-state geometry, latent-task posterior inference, and OOD extrapolation.","experiments":"The experiments are synthetic and intentionally small. The linear regression setup uses D=6 weights and Gaussian observation noise ε~N(0,0.25). The Markov setting uses a vocabulary/state size V=6. The reported qualitative findings are that, on in-distribution sequences, hidden-state components along task-vector directions converge toward the true task vector as sequence length grows. On OOD sequences, the model can use context-related statistics in nearly orthogonal subspaces, and those subspaces emerge only when the training task diversity is sufficiently large. The provided full text does not include the complete quantitative tables or exact performance numbers, so the evidence should be read as mechanistic rather than as a scaling claim.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–45 minutes. Start with Section 4 for the formal definition of task vectors and the two inference modes. Then read Section 5 for the in-distribution Bayesian retrieval picture, especially how hidden-state coefficients evolve with context length. Section 6 is the most relevant part for pretraining intuition, because it links OOD generalization to a near-orthogonal context-statistics subspace. Section 7 is useful for understanding failure cases of heuristic task-vector definitions. The synthetic setting is narrow, but the conceptual separation between retrieval geometry and extrapolation geometry is the main takeaway.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"重点看第4节的任务向量几何结构分析，可加深对Transformer ICL和泛化机制的理解。","en":"Focus on the task vector geometry analysis in section 4, which can deepen the understanding of Transformer ICL and generalization mechanisms."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Wisconsin–Madison"],"authors":["Hao Yan","Haolin Yang","Yiqiao Zhong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["transformer interpretability","task vector","in-context learning","ood generalization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03780","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.03780","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.03354","title":"What Happens Inside Agent Memory? 上出错。论文要解决的是：这些阶段在模型内部是否对应可分离、可追踪的计算电路（circuits）？这些电路如何随模型规模涌现？能否据此把端到端错误定位到具体阶段，从而做诊断而不是只看一个总分？","importance":"对基座研究的价值主要在训练动态/机制层面：\n- 给出一个与 scaling 相关的具体现象：在 Qwen-3 家族上出现“control-before-content”不对称——小模型已具备路由/控制相关的因果电路，但内容抽取与 grounding 的可检测电路要到更大规模才出现。这对“用小模型跑 agent 框架”的风险评估很直接：外部行为看起来会路由，但可能在写入与读取内容上系统性失真。\n- 把多次调用的 agent pipeline 纳入 mechanistic LongMemEval（500 问、5 类 memory）。检索本身是 embedding search，不走 LLM。\n2）把每个 memory 操作当成独立 forward pass：Write（从对话抽取事实）、Manage（对新事实与 top-5 邻居做 add/update/delete/none 决策）、Read（基于检索到的 top-5 事实回答）。这样可以把“阶段”定义成可对齐的计算单元。\n3）Feature circuit tracing：使用预训练线性转码器 PLT（把每层 MLP 替换为稀疏 encoder-decoder）得到更接近单语义的 feature 激活；在此表示上构建从输入 token→特征→输出 logit hub：在较晚层出现一个共享的“枢纽”特征群，Write 与 Read 都会用到；但是否形成可用的 grounding 方向，取决于阶段提示词/框架如何“招募”这块共享底座。\n- 诊断而非干预：作者尝试直接放大内容电路做 steer/intervention，发现跨阶段 pipeline 中很脆弱，于是转向把电路分离性用于 read-out geomet","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-04T22:24:34+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03196.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个轻量可靠性信号：在生成之前，仅根据 hidden states 的几何位置，判断一个输入是否可能不可回答。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在表示层面。论文显示，某些不可回答性不是只在输出阶段或拒答策略中出现，而是在早层 hidden states 中已经形成几何偏离。更具体地说，数学问题中的不可回答样本会偏离可回答样本的表示中心，而事实类问题没有稳定信号。这提示 answerability 不是一个统一的模型属性，它与任务形式、输入结构和对齐策略都有关系。对基座模型研究来说，这有助于区分两类问题：模型是否编码了“不该回答”的结构信息，以及 post-train 是否把这种信息转化成拒答或谨慎回答。","technical_approach":"方法很简单。作者先构造可回答样本的参考集合，取 hidden states 的均值作为 answerable centroid；对新输入，计算其 hidden state 与这个中心的余弦距离，距离越远则越可能不可回答。为减弱 Transformer 表示的各向异性，方法使用 mean-centering。实验设计使用 matched pairs：每个不可回答提示都匹配一个同领域、同长度、同句法形式的可回答提示，只改变导致不可回答的属性。这样可以尽量排除表面形式差异。分析覆盖不同层，并比较不同任务形式：Math、Fact、Code。方法不需要失败样本标签、不需要模型输出、不需要采样，也不需要训练 probe。它与 PRISM、MHAD 这类监督内部状态检测不同，后者依赖标注的幻觉或事实性信号。","experiments":"实验使用三个 7–8B instruction-tuned 模型：Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct 和 Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3。数学提示上，几何偏离能稳定区分不可回答输入，ROC-AUC 为 0.78–0.84，超过简单 refusal baseline，也优于需要 5 倍推理成本的 multi-sample self-consistency baseline。事实类提示上，三种模型都没有显著几何信号，说明该方法不能直接泛化到所有知识问答。代码提示上，三个模型都有较大 effect size，但在当前样本量下显著性不稳定。层分析显示信号在早层最强，通常集中在第 2–5 层，越接近输出层越弱。模型行为也不同：同样的几何 outlier，Qwen 更可能拒答，Llama 不一定拒答，说明 alignment 决定了模型如何使用这些内部信号。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 15–25 分钟。重点看方法部分的 centroid distance 定义、mean-centering 处理和 matched-pair 构造；再看分任务结果表，尤其是 Math 有信号、Fact 无信号、Code 不稳定这三个边界。层级分析图值得看，因为“早层最强”是最有研究含义的观察。读的时候应保留一个限制：这种方法依赖任务形式较规整、可回答参考集容易获得的场景；如果输入分布混杂，几何距离可能首先编码 task form，而不是 answerability。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether hidden-state geometry can provide a pre-generation signal of answerability, without using model outputs, labeled failure data, or a trained probe.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is diagnostic. The results suggest that some forms of unanswerability are encoded early in the representation geometry, before decoding and before explicit refusal behavior. The signal is not universal: it is reliable for mathematical prompts, absent for factual prompts, and suggestive but less statistically stable for code. This distinction is useful for separating representation-level knowledge from alignment-level action. A model may internally mark an input as structurally out-of-distribution while its post-training policy may or may not turn that signal into refusal.","technical_approach":"The method builds an answerable reference set and computes a centroid in hidden-state space. A new prompt is scored by cosine distance from that centroid after mean-centering, which reduces the dominant-direction anisotropy common in LLM representations. The design uses matched answerable and unanswerable prompt pairs that share domain, length, and syntactic form, changing only the property that makes the prompt unanswerable. This is meant to isolate answerability from surface form. The analysis is layer-wise and task-form-specific, covering Math, Fact, and Code. Unlike supervised hallucination detectors such as PRISM or MHAD, the method does not train a classifier on labeled failures and does not require generated text.","experiments":"The experiments cover Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3. On mathematical prompts, centroid deviation separates unanswerable from answerable inputs with ROC-AUC between 0.78 and 0.84 across the three models. It outperforms a simple refusal-based baseline and compares favorably to a five-sample self-consistency baseline with higher inference cost. On factual prompts, no significant signal appears in any model. On code prompts, effect sizes are large across models, but statistical significance is mixed at the studied sample size. Layer-wise results show the strongest signal in early layers, around layers 2–5, and a general decline toward the output layer. The same geometric outliers can lead to different behaviors: Qwen is more likely to refuse, while Llama may not, indicating that alignment changes how the model acts on the signal.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 15–25 minutes. Focus on the centroid-distance method, the mean-centering step, and the matched-pair construction. Then read the task-form results, because the boundary between Math, Fact, and Code is the main empirical point. The layer-wise plots are the most relevant part for researchers interested in representation formation. The main caveat is that geometry can encode task form more strongly than answerability; the approach is most plausible when the query format is controlled and an answerable reference set is available.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读数学/事实对比那组结果；它提醒别把 hidden geometry 的不确定性信号误当通用能力。","en":"Read the math-vs-fact comparison; it is a useful warning against treating hidden-state geometry as a universal uncertainty signal."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yucheng Du"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["representation-geometry","answerability","hallucination","uncertainty-estimation","pre-generation-signal"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03196","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.03196","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.03907","title":"Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting","abstract":"Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. 维度上呈现强烈的“非均匀性”：少数 token 位置被强干预，大多数位置几乎不动；而文献中常见的 Const family（ActAdd/CAA/ITI 等）假设对所有位置用同一个 α，天然不匹配。\n据此提出 Prompt Steering Replacement（PSR）：仍然使用 rank-1 方向（一个 steering vector z_attr），但把系数从常数 α 变成 token-specific 的 α_i，并且 α_i 由当前 token 的激活（或其可计算特征）预测出来。训练目标是“模仿 prompt steering 造成的激活变化/或输出行为”，相当于把 prompt 的复杂、不可解释的内部干预蒸馏到一个小而可解释的 gating/回归模块上。\n论文还讨论了 PSR 何时能表示 prompt steering：需要 prompt 的主要作用在某层附近可被近似为 rank-1 方向 + 位置相关强度（而不是多方向、多机制叠加）。作者承认该假设对所有指令不成立，但认为 token-wise 系数是更一般理论的必要成分。","experiments":"给出的全文片段未包含完整实验表格与具体数值，因此无法可靠复述“提升多少、在哪些模型/基准上”的精确数字。就方法设计与实验设置层面，论文强调：\n- 任务是 long-form Global rule-extraction methods typically learn symbolic surrogates without grounding rules in model circuitry, while mechanistic interpretability can connect behaviors to neuron sets but often depends on hand-crafted hypotheses and expensive neuron-level interventions. We introduce MechaRule, a pipeline that grounds rule extraction in LLM circui","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-04T18:27:37+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_03058.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是：能否把 LLM 的某类行为规则，用可读的符号规则表达出来，同时把这些规则锚定到真正有因果作用的内部神经元上。现有 rule extraction 往往只能给输入到输出的符号近似，但不知道模型内部是谁在实现这条规则；mechanistic interpretability 则能做局部因果干预，但通常依赖手工假设、逐神经元扫描，成本高且不系统。MechaRule 试图把这两件事接起来：先自动找出能区分成功/失败样本的规则，再用这些规则引导层次化 group ablation，定位少量对该行为有控制作用的神经元，并为这些神经元附上规则解释。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接影响不如训练方法论文大，但对机制分析和训练后诊断有一定启发。它的价值在于提出一种较完整的『符号规则 ↔ 因果电路』对齐流程，而不是只做相关性解释。对预训练研究员来说，这类方法有两个潜在用途：一是分析模型在特定能力上到底依赖哪些稀疏启发式，而不是抽象地说“模型学会了规则”；二是为数据配比、持续训练、能力修复提供更细粒度的诊断工具。不过要注意，这篇工作更偏 interpretability pipeline，不是直接改进预训练目标或 scaling 的论文，启发主要在分析方法层面。","technical_approach":"MechaRule 的技术路线分两步。\n\n第一步是 task-local rule extraction。作者先定义一个任务数据集和 success metric，把样本划分为模型成功与失败两类。然后构造一个可解释特征空间：由一个 LLM agent 查看对比样本，提出候选谓词，例如某个操作数是否能被 3 整除、结果是否落在某个区间等；再结合少量人工 seed features，筛出最能区分 success/failure 的特征。基于这些特征，使用 RuleSHAP 提取稀疏 if-then 规则。这里的关键不是做全局通用解释，而是围绕某个任务行为建立局部、可读、可操作的规则假设。\n\n第二步是 neuron localization with contrastive hierarchical ablation。作者不做昂贵的逐神经元穷举，而是利用两个经验假设：\n1. sparse gating：某条行为规则通常对应少量关键神经元；\n2. monotone/saturating group effects：在固定 baseline/flip regime 下，多个神经元的干预效果可能重叠、饱和，甚至被少数 dominant neurons 掩盖，作者称之为 overtopping。\n\n基于这两个假设，方法把候选神经元组织成层次化 group，先做 group ablation，再只向效果足够大的分支继续下钻。剪枝标准不是简单加和，而是统计上高置信的 below-threshold effect：如果一个 group 的整体干预效果都不够大，就认为这条分支不值得继续搜索。这样可以在存在非加性、重叠和支配效应时，仍然较高效地定位 causally influential neurons。\n\n最后，作者把抽取出的规则与定位出的神经元做锚定，形成 neuron-anchored rules：规则预测什么样的输入会触发该神经元对行为的控制，神经元干预则提供因果验证。相比传统 rule extraction，它多了内部机制落点；相比纯 mechanistic probing，它减少了手工猜 feature 的负担。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，实验重点不是大规模 benchmark 刷分，而是验证这条 pipeline 是否能在结构化任务上同时做到三件事：规则可读、神经元稀疏、干预有效。作者强调的实证现象包括：\n- 在固定干预 regime 下，少量 agonist neurons 的中和就能显著破坏某类规则相关行为，支持 sparse gating 假设；\n- group-level effect 呈现近似单调和饱和特征，说明层次化 group ablation 比逐点搜索更合适；\n- 存在 dominant neurons 或 catastrophic antagonists，会在大尺度干预时掩盖较弱但更具体的神经元，因此需要自适应下钻而不是一次性大范围 ablation；\n- 自动发现的规则可以预测哪些样本会被特定神经元控制，说明符号规则与内部电路之间不是纯相关，而有一定可验证对应关系。\n\n不过，用户提供的正文片段没有包含完整实验章节、具体模型规模、任务集合、命中率、ablation 成本下降比例等数字，因此不能补写具体数值。精读时应重点核对：规则预测精度、定位到的神经元数量、相对 exhaustive ablation 的计算节省、以及 intervention 后行为翻转率。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题去读。建议顺序如下：\n1. 先看 Introduction，把作者要连接的两个对象想清楚：symbolic rule extraction 和 mechanistic localization。\n2. 重点看方法部分对 H1/H2 两个假设的定义，尤其是 overtopping、monotone、saturating 这些词在实验里是怎么 operationalize 的。\n3. 看 task-local feature discovery 与 RuleSHAP 的结合方式。这里决定了规则是否真的可读，还是只是另一层黑盒。\n4. 再看 hierarchical ablation 的搜索与剪枝准则。这是方法效率的核心，也是和传统逐神经元 probing 的主要区别。\n5. 最后看案例图，确认某条规则、某组样本、某个神经元之间的对应关系是否足够具体。\n\n预计阅读时间 25–35 分钟。如果你主要做预训练，这篇不必逐式推导，但值得关注它如何把行为规则和内部稀疏单元联系起来；这对分析 tokenizer 诱导的启发式、数据偏置形成的局部电路，可能有后续用途。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a long-standing gap between symbolic explanation and mechanistic explanation in LLMs. Rule-extraction methods can produce human-readable decision rules, but those rules are usually not grounded in the model’s internal circuitry. Mechanistic interpretability can identify causal neurons, but often requires expensive neuron-by-neuron interventions and hand-crafted hypotheses. MechaRule aims to connect the two: extract task-local symbolic rules, then use them to efficiently localize sparse neurons that causally control the corresponding behavior.","importance":"This is more of an interpretability and diagnosis paper than a pretraining-method paper, but it is still relevant to foundation-model research. Its main contribution is a pipeline for linking behavior-level rules to internal causal components, rather than stopping at correlation. For pretraining researchers, that matters as a diagnostic tool: it can help reveal whether a capability is implemented by a small set of brittle heuristics, how data biases become localized circuits, and where targeted repair or continued training might act. The impact is therefore indirect but meaningful for understanding training outcomes.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"$2f","reading_guide":"Read this paper for the method design, not for benchmark numbers. Start with the introduction to understand the exact gap it is trying to close: symbolic rule extraction versus mechanistic localization. Then focus on the two core assumptions, especially sparse gating and overtopping, because the whole search strategy depends on them. Next, inspect how the LLM-agent feature proposal and RuleSHAP interact; this determines whether the symbolic side is genuinely interpretable or just another learned proxy. After that, study the hierarchical ablation procedure and its pruning rule, since that is the main efficiency contribution. The case studies are also important: they show whether a specific rule, a subset of examples, and a localized neuron really line up in a concrete way.\n\nEstimated reading time: 25–35 minutes.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可解释性领域的增量改进，仅需阅读神经元定位策略的相关章节即可。","en":"Incremental improvement in mechanistic interpretability, only the neuron localization strategy section is worth reading."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University Department of Informatics"],"authors":["Francesco Sovrano","Gabriele Dominici","Marc Langheinrich"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic interpretability","rule extraction","neuron ablation","LLM explainability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03058","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.03058","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.03160","title":"Pairwise matrices for sparse autoencoders: single-feature inspection mislabels causal axes","abstract":"The standard sparse-autoencoder (SAE) interpretability protocol labels each feature from its top-activating contexts and validates by single-feature steering. It reframes ZO fine-tuning from high-dimensional gradient estimation in parameter space to approximate learning dynamics in function space. This is useful for memory-constrained adaptation, black-box tuning, and communication-limited optimization, where ZO methods are practically attractive but poorly explained by worst-case convergence bounds.","technical_approach":"The paper derives the one-step learning dynamics of ZO-SGD and shows that an empirical NTK-like object naturally appears. The induced ZO-eNTK can be viewed as the standard first-order eNTK projected onto a random low-dimensional subspace spanned by perturbation vectors. In this view, ZO does not need to recover the full parameter gradient accurately. It only needs to preserve the relevant inner products between neural tangent features in function space. The Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma is then used to justify why this random projection can approximate the first-order dynamics without an explicit dependence on the full parameter dimension. The analysis also explains why Gaussian and Rademacher perturbations behave similarly: the number of perturbations matters more than the exact perturbation distribution.","experiments":"The experimental evidence is intended to check how well ZO-eNTK approximates FO-eNTK and how that approximation changes with the number of perturbations, perturbation distribution, model dimension, and output dimension. From the provided full-content excerpt, the reliable takeaways are: increasing the number of perturbations improves kernel fidelity; Gaussian and Rademacher perturbations are both sufficient in practice; and the relevant scaling is argued to depend on output vocabulary size V rather than parameter dimension d. 输出”而不是让主代理学习更强的长上下文消化能力。\n2）Terminus-4B 模型：以 Qwen3-4B 为底座，先用内部使用遥测收集的终端执行轨迹做 SFT，让模型学会基本的命令选择、错误解读与总结格式；再用 GRPO 做 RL。这里的重点不是探索新 RL 算法，而是把 rollout 组织成“子代理任务可复现”的训练单元。\n3）子代理专用 RL pipeline + rubric-based LLM-as-judge：为降低成本并减少对前沿模型依赖，rollout 时尽量弱化主代理作用，甚至用 vanilla Qwen3-4B 充当主代理，使每次 rollout 从同一问题起点开始，便于对比候选轨迹。奖励不是简单的成功/失败，而是把候选子代理轨迹与参考轨迹对齐，在多个质量维度与失败模式上打分（例如：是否抓住关键错误行、是否产生幻觉式总结、是否遗漏关键信息、是否采取无效命令循环等），形成更密的训练信号。","experiments":"论文宣称的关键结果有三类：\n- 端到端基准：在 SWE-Bench Pro 与内部 SWE-Bench C# 上，把 本身。这对研究“后训练信号到底在教什么”有参考价值，但贡献更多是工程配方层面的，不是基础范式推进。","technical_approach":"方法很直接，核心是三项数据合成改动，然后在一个现成 thinking 基座上做单阶段 SFT。第一，扩大任务合成时使用的知识图谱或证据子图规模，把原来较小的局部图扩展成更大的 evidence subgraph。这样生成的问题更容易要求多节点证据聚合，而不是单跳检索。第二，扩充工具集，让 agent 在 ReAct 轨迹中接触更多工具类型，学习更丰富的交互模式。第三，做严格的 low-step filtering，把工具调用步数低于阈值 T_min 的轨迹直接丢弃，强行抬高训练集难度下限。最终只保留约 10k 条高难轨迹，对 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 做标准 SFT。方法上的关键判断是：与其在训练算法上继续加 RL，不如先把监督数据从“可解”提升到“必须长程搜索才能解”。这也是它与很多 agent 工作的区别，后者常把重点放在框架、记忆或工具编排，而这里主要押注数据分布。","experiments":"论文报告的主结果是：仅用约 10.6k 条高难轨迹做一次 SFT，就让 30B 级模型在多个搜索/agent benchmark 上取得很强表现。文中给出的数字包括：BrowseComp 46.0%，BrowseComp-ZH 58.1%，Humanity’s Last Exam 34.6%，xbench 78.0%。作者还拿 Tongyi DeepResearch 这类采用 CPT+SFT+RL 流水线的工业系统做对比，后者对应成绩为 43.4%、46.7%、32.9%、75.0%，OpenSeeker-v2 在这些基准上均有领先。对阅读者更有价值的是其隐含结论：数据压缩得很小，但难度和信息密度被显著抬高后，SFT 的能力上限比很多人预期更高。可惜从给出的全文片段看，消融细节还不够充分，尤其是三项数据改动各自的边际贡献、不同 T_min 的敏感性、以及是否存在 benchmark-specific overfitting，这些都需要在正文表格里进一步核实。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐段细抠。先看 Introduction，明确作者是在挑战“强搜索 agent 必须依赖 CPT+RL”的常见配方。然后直接看方法部分的三项数据改动：graph size、tool set、low-step filtering，这三点就是全文主线。实验部分重点看主表和消融，尤其要确认提升到底来自更大工具集、更多证据图，还是简单地把容易样本删掉。若只想判断是否值得跟进，15 到 20 分钟足够。若关心 post-train 数据工程，可进一步检查训练集构造细节、轨迹长度分布和 benchmark 泄漏风险。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This work is not about pretraining in the narrow sense. It tackles a post-training question for search agents: can a strong deep-search agent be built with plain supervised fine-tuning alone, without the heavy industrial recipe of continual pretraining plus RL, if the training trajectories are sufficiently informative and difficult? The paper argues yes, and places the burden on data quality rather than on a more complex optimization pipeline.","importance":"Its direct relevance to foundation-model pretraining is limited, since the main contribution is in agent trajectory synthesis rather than training objectives, scaling laws, or optimization dynamics. Still, it offers a useful signal for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. It suggests that some capabilities often attributed to RL or multi-stage training may in practice come from the difficulty distribution and informational richness of the supervision. That is a meaningful observation if one is trying to disentangle what post-training stages actually teach. The contribution is therefore more about data-centric post-training design than about core LM methodology.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"The headline result is that a single SFT run on roughly 10.6k hard trajectories is enough to produce a strong 30B-scale search agent. Reported benchmark numbers are 46.0% on BrowseComp, 58.1% on BrowseComp-ZH, 34.6% on Humanity’s Last Exam, and 78.0% on xbench. The paper compares these results against industrial systems such as Tongyi DeepResearch, which reportedly uses a CPT+SFT+RL pipeline and scores 43.4%, 46.7%, 32.9%, and 75.0% on the same benchmarks. The interesting takeaway is not only the absolute scores, but the compression of supervision: a very small dataset, if made hard enough, can push SFT much further than expected.
